Unlogic’s Optimum Optimill MH50V LinuxCNC conversion

268

Unlogic’s Optimum Optimill MH50V LinuxCNC conversion

Partial contact evidence

Main wiring diagram · SmoothieBox

Relay ratings below are corrected from Panasonic catalog pages 81–82; the main drawing now includes the current canonical source graph; fitted hardware remains unqualified.

Why this controller: Original machine uses separate motor power stages. Keep their motor-power/phase/encoder wiring; connect SmoothieBox exterior command contacts through the shown qualified interfaces. Chapter 18 is a proposed case harness, not an as-built rating certificate.

Selected architecture: SmoothieBox exterior STEP/DIR contacts feed differential transmitters into the Delta DB44 peripherals. Each axis has a separate isolated ENABLE stage; retain motor/encoder/brake/STO power domains and qualify the spindle and cabinet interfaces independently.

Original machine uses separate motor power stages. Keep their motor-power/phase/encoder wiring; connect SmoothieBox exterior command contacts through the shown qualified interfaces. Chapter 18 is a proposed case harness, not an as-built rating certificate. Selected architecture: SmoothieBox exterior case contacts with retained external power stages. The main diagram and connection-ID schedule define the current proposed routes. Dotted GUESS routes need the named commissioning checks; OPEN routes remain disconnected until the missing terminal, rating or interface is established. Previous Core alternatives remain only in the superseded historical architecture record.

Current installation sequence
  1. Match the actual machine and controller revisions, trace the unpowered harness and verify all signal/reference, power and independent safety boundaries before energising. Follow the current connection IDs rather than the superseded architecture prose.
  2. Controller logic supply · separate from motor power: SmoothieBox exterior contact assignment is a proposed Chapter 18 harness; verify the fitted case and logic level. Verify exact board supply path and rail ratings; motor current uses separate wiring and fuses.
  3. MH50V X axis · SmoothieBox differential STEP/DIR into DB44 peripheral: SmoothieBox exterior contact assignment is a proposed Chapter 18 harness; verify the fitted case and logic level. This is the requested peripheral topology: Smoothie SmoothieBox -> line-driver interface -> external DB44 connector -> Delta drive CN1. Delta B3A-L pin table identifies these four contacts; only SmoothieBox axis mapping and the proposed interface/cable hookup are guessed. Do not populate PULL_HI_S pin35 or PULL_HI_P pin36 for differential mode.
  4. MH50V Y axis · SmoothieBox differential STEP/DIR into DB44 peripheral: SmoothieBox exterior contact assignment is a proposed Chapter 18 harness; verify the fitted case and logic level. This is the requested peripheral topology: Smoothie SmoothieBox -> line-driver interface -> external DB44 connector -> Delta drive CN1. Delta B3A-L pin table identifies these four contacts; only SmoothieBox axis mapping and the proposed interface/cable hookup are guessed. Do not populate PULL_HI_S pin35 or PULL_HI_P pin36 for differential mode.
  5. MH50V Z axis · SmoothieBox differential STEP/DIR into DB44 peripheral: SmoothieBox exterior contact assignment is a proposed Chapter 18 harness; verify the fitted case and logic level. This is the requested peripheral topology: Smoothie SmoothieBox -> line-driver interface -> external DB44 connector -> Delta drive CN1. Delta B3A-L pin table identifies these four contacts; only SmoothieBox axis mapping and the proposed interface/cable hookup are guessed. Do not populate PULL_HI_S pin35 or PULL_HI_P pin36 for differential mode.
  6. MH50V X servo · model-specific power and motor boundary: SmoothieBox exterior contact assignment is a proposed Chapter 18 harness; verify the fitted case and logic level. Owner reports a 750 W X motor; this is not a verified Delta drive nameplate. Identify the exact ASDA-B3 suffix and motor model before assigning AC input requirements, terminal layout, PE, U/V/W, encoder connector, brake or STO. Terminal marks below describe the B3 family boundary where applicable; all conductors stay OPEN until matched to the installed model/manual and cabinet schedule.
  7. MH50V Y servo · model-specific power and motor boundary: SmoothieBox exterior contact assignment is a proposed Chapter 18 harness; verify the fitted case and logic level. Owner reports a 750 W Y motor; this is not a verified Delta drive nameplate. Identify the exact ASDA-B3 suffix and motor model before assigning AC input requirements, terminal layout, PE, U/V/W, encoder connector, brake or STO. Terminal marks below describe the B3 family boundary where applicable; all conductors stay OPEN until matched to the installed model/manual and cabinet schedule.
  8. MH50V Z servo · model-specific power and motor boundary: SmoothieBox exterior contact assignment is a proposed Chapter 18 harness; verify the fitted case and logic level. Owner reports a 1 kW Z motor; this is not a verified Delta drive nameplate. Identify the exact ASDA-B3 suffix and motor model before assigning AC input requirements, terminal layout, PE, U/V/W, encoder connector, brake or STO. Terminal marks below describe the B3 family boundary where applicable; all conductors stay OPEN until matched to the installed model/manual and cabinet schedule.
  9. MH50V X servo · individual CN1 DB44 low-voltage contacts: SmoothieBox exterior contact assignment is a proposed Chapter 18 harness; verify the fitted case and logic level. DB44 is the machine-side peripheral connector into this Delta drive. Contact functions are from the B3A-L CN1 pin table and remain conditional on matching the exact drive suffix. Each contact is separately exposed. Owner assigns Alarm/Alarm Reset only on Z; X/Y DI/DO pins have no owner function assignment. The root-owned shared isolated SON stage may connect here only after separate DI supply/common domains are checked; do not parallel pin 9 across drives until each COM+ supply and return are proven common. The dedicated per-axis ENABLE and COM+ circuits below supersede the old shared SON proposal. Alarm/reset isolation remains a separate unresolved peripheral interface; the interface labels are not SmoothieBox physical terminals.
  10. MH50V Y servo · individual CN1 DB44 low-voltage contacts: SmoothieBox exterior contact assignment is a proposed Chapter 18 harness; verify the fitted case and logic level. DB44 is the machine-side peripheral connector into this Delta drive. Contact functions are from the B3A-L CN1 pin table and remain conditional on matching the exact drive suffix.   11. MH50V Z servo · individual CN1 DB44 low-voltage contacts: SmoothieBox exterior contact assignment is a proposed Chapter 18 harness; verify the fitted case and logic level.   12. Later 2 kW Delta spindle servo · separate command and motor stage: Owner later replaces stock VFD/gearbox with a 2 kW 400 V Delta B3 spindle servo. Owner three-axis DB44 drawing is not a spindle terminal map. Identify installed suffix, command mode, speed scaling, spindle run/stop and feedback. Smoothie firmware support for a pulse-command spindle is not established; retain existing compatible spindle controller if unsupported.
  13. Proximity homing sensors and cabinet hardware · individual functions: Owner reports homing-switch damage and names ABB SSR10 safety relay, Schneider contactors, coolant, mist, operator buttons and TouchDRO. No complete sensor/IO terminal map is supplied. Powered proximity sensors require rated supply and matched NPN/PNP isolation/conditioning; dry-contact wiring cannot be assumed. Retain independent safety/drive-ready latch and matched power circuits.
  14. X independent servo ENABLE · isolated LED input stage: Independent X ENABLE control, not STO/E-stop. Configure correct drive DI ServoOn and case output polarity; prove reset/loss-of-command is off. SmoothieBox exterior contact assignment is a proposed Chapter 18 harness; verify the fitted case and logic level. New engineering proposal: SmoothieBox GPIO → ULN2003AN → AQY212GS LED, isolated contact pins3/4 to configured machine run circuit. This slow process interface does not implement E-stop, STO or PWM. Verify startup inactive, receiver mode/current, isolation layout and loaded off-state leakage before use. Nominal 390 Ω limits LED to approximately 5.5–9 mA using conservative 4.75 V supply, 1.5 V LED and 1.1 V sink drop assumptions; recheck tolerance/temperature and actual sink voltage. Panasonic catalog printed pages 81–82 lists AQY212GS output absolute maxima of 60 V peak AC/DC and 1.0 A at 25°C, with power and temperature derating; these are not unconditional working ratings. Qualify receiver current, off-state leakage, isolation layout and the actual fitted device. Contact is bidirectional and galvanically isolated from the LED side: no logic ground crosses that barrier.
  15. X isolated servo-on contact · drive-side reference remains isolated: Contact is optically driven by U6_X LED in the preceding axis enable stage. SmoothieBox exterior contact assignment is a proposed Chapter 18 harness; verify the fitted case and logic level. New shared servo-enable proposal. All three DI circuits must use this verified 24V supply/reference. Do not join unrelated supply returns. Verify DI1 Servo On parameter on each drive, loaded input current and off leakage. CN1 signal GND29/40 are separately accounted for and are not automatically the isolated DI return. Retain independent STO/hardware stop and drive fault handling; this GPIO is not a safety function.

  • SmoothieBox Chapter 18 field-contact drawing: Defines proposed exterior STEP/DIR/ENABLE, ground and accessory contact names. These field contacts are not Core header numbers. The drawing does not certify an assembled case or its output ratings.
  • Delta Electronics ASDA-B3 Servo Drive User Manual §3.1.3, printed p.3-6 (manufacturer-authored mirrored PDF): The 220 V-series ≤1.5 kW single-phase figure feeds main R/T through MC, leaves S unused, feeds L1C/L2C ahead of MC, shows U/V/W to motor and drive/motor PE, and places MCCB and a noise filter upstream. It does not establish the fitted MH50V hardware.
  • Delta ASDA-B3 manual §3.1.3 p.3-6 single-phase figure: Direct source reference is attached to this exact conductor; verify fitted applicability.

X independent servo ENABLE · isolated LED input stageU5_X · TI ULN2003AN · DIP16 · 9 · COM · NC (noninductive LED)No conductor drawn. Independent X ENABLE control, not STO/E-stop. Configure correct drive DI ServoOn and case output polarity; prove reset/loss-of-command is off. SmoothieBox exterior contact assignment is a proposed Chapter 18 harness; verify the fitted case and logic level. New engineering proposal: SmoothieBox GPIO → ULN2003AN → AQY212GS LED, isolated contact pins3/4 to configured machine run circuit. This slow process interface does not implement E-stop, STO or PWM. Verify startup inactive, receiver mode/current, isolation layout and loaded off-state leakage before use. Nominal 390 Ω limits LED to approximately 5.5–9 mA using conservative 4.75 V supply, 1.5 V LED and 1.1 V sink drop assumptions; recheck tolerance/temperature and actual sink voltage. Panasonic catalog printed pages 81–82 lists AQY212GS output absolute maxima of 60 V peak AC/DC and 1.0 A at 25°C, with power and temperature derating; these are not unconditional working ratings. Qualify receiver current, off-state leakage, isolation layout and the actual fitted device. Contact is bidirectional and galvanically isolated from the LED side: no logic ground crosses that barrier.

The earlier circuit details remain immediately below.

Earlier alternative retrofit architecture and circuit details

Owner retrofit guide · Unlogic's Optimum Optimill MH50V

The owner’s cabinet schedule places DB44 connectors as external machine peripherals between the controller and Delta servo drives; the SmoothieBox/Core feeds into those peripheral connectors through differential line-driver ICs. The Delta B3A-L manual confirms pins 37/39 SIGN−/+, 41/43 PULSE−/+ and warns not to apply 24V to pulse/sign pins. The proposed AM26LV31E circuit explicitly shows every IC signal, supply, ground and enable pin. Axis/channel pairing remains dotted until drive labels and cable continuity are checked.

Which Smoothie target fits this machine?
  • SmoothieBox: Use a SmoothieBox enclosure with V2 Core. It must expose Core J5 STEP/DIR and 3.3V/GND connections to the differential line-driver board; it does not replace the servo drive power stages.
  • V2 Core: Recommended control target: V2 Core plus two TI AM26LV31E chips (or a reviewed rated isolated differential module) to drive the B3A-L differential pulse/sign pins. Validate voltage, line common, pulse mode, output power budget and cable fault behavior.
  • V2 Prime: Prime integrated stepper stages cannot replace the Delta AC servo drives. Prime GPIO breakout is not established as equivalent to the proposed Core differential drive interface; retain servo drives and use a suitable Core-based control interface.
Retain or add
  • Two TI AM26LV31E quad 3.3V differential line drivers for six STEP/DIR channels; verify Core J5.10 current budget or use a separate regulated 3.3V supply. Each chip local 0.1µF decoupling, G pin4 high and /G pin12 low.
  • Three shielded twisted differential pairs per axis as a reviewed cable design; owner used stranded shielded Cat6 in this installation and reports a short that damaged the original DIR output, so inspect/continuity-test before reuse.
  • Isolated, 24V-rated digital output/input modules only after exact B3A-L DI/DO parameter and NPN/PNP mode is confirmed; pin 9 default SON is not a safety-rated input.
  • Retain exact Delta B3 drives/motors, encoder cables, Z brake circuit, STO/E-stop relay/contactors and machine earth.
  • Shared isolated servo-on proposal: ULN2003AN DIP16, AQY212GS SOP4, 390Ω LED resistor, 10kΩ pull-down; verified shared 24V DI supply. Fault/STO/brake remain independent model-matched circuits.
Conversion and commissioning
  1. Read the model suffix on all X/Y/Z/spindle Delta drives. Confirm they are B3A-L pulse-type drives; the same-family pin numbers are not portable to other variants.
  2. With power isolated, check every DB44 conductor for continuity, shorts between differential pair members and shorts to shields; the owner reported a crossed/melted X DIR pair that damaged a Mesa output and proximity switch.
  3. Implement AM26LV31E diff outputs with exact pin assignments shown. Verify VCC/GND, both enable polarities, idle state and pulse pair with oscilloscope before connecting drives; test one axis while mechanically uncoupled/drive disabled.
  4. Set/read back P1.000 differential command mode and pulse format. Do not connect 24V PULL_HI pins 35/36 in line-driver mode; never apply 24V to SIGN/PULSE pair contacts.
  5. Read back DI/DO assignments and polarity before enabling SON/alarm/reset. Confirm safe E-stop/STO, limit switches and proximity inputs independently; do not repeat unattended homing until fault cause is corrected.
  6. Keep servo AC, U/V/W, encoder, brake, STO and protective earth in the exact Delta machine wiring domain, never on Smoothie pins.

Pin-by-pin schedule and commissioning notes
  • Owner wiring image (machine-side DB44 peripheral): X/Y/Z each label pin37 SIGN−, pin39 SIGN+, pin41 PULSE−, pin43 PULSE+. Owner also labels Z pin27 Alarm, pin33 Alarm Reset, pin9 servo-on; these are owner-assigned roles, not generic pin function.
  • Delta ASDA-B3 manual Rev? section 3.3.3 pp.3-71–72, pulse-type B3A-L CN1: pin1 DO4+,2 DO3−,3 DO3+,4 DO2−,5 DO2+,6 DO1−,7 DO1+,8 DI4−,9 DI1−,10 DI2−,11 COM+ (24V ±10%),12 DI9−,13 OZ,14 MON2,15 DO6−,16 DO6+,17 MON1,18 T_REF,19 GND,20 V_REF,21 OA,22 /OA,23 /OB,24 /OZ,25 OB,26 DO4−,27 DO5−,28 DO5+,29 GND,30 DI8−,31 DI7−,32 DI6−,33 DI5−,34 DI3−,35 PULL_HI_S (24V for open-collector sign),36 PULL_HI_P (24V for open-collector pulse),37 SIGN−,38 NC,39 SIGN+,40 GND,41 PULSE−,42 NC,43 PULSE+,44 OCZ. Do not connect NC pins38/42; never apply 24V to pins37/39/41/43. Differential line-driver input up to 4Mpps per Delta manual; open-collector requires 24V pull-high and is a different circuit.
  • AM26LV31E exact SOIC/TSSOP-16 pins: 1 1A,2 1Y,3 1Z,4 G active-high enable,5 2Z,6 2Y,7 2A,8 GND,9 3A,10 3Y,11 3Z,12 /G active-low enable,13 4Z,14 4Y,15 4A,16 VCC. Outputs Y/Z are complementary pair; confirm pin polarity with scope.
  • Core source contacts: J5.16 STEP_A/.18 DIR_A, .22 STEP_B/.24 DIR_B, .28 STEP_C/.30 DIR_C, .34 STEP_D/.36 DIR_D; J5.10 3V3, grounds .19/.20/.25/.26/.32/.37/.38/.39; J35.6 PWM, .7 TTL candidate, .10 GND. Slash net names are not active-low assertion evidence.
  • The owner’s Mesa board identity conflict (7i96S/7i84 vs later 7i76S) is historical. The current retrofit proposal does not use Mesa contacts. Servo AC supply, motor phase connector, encoder connector, brake pins, STO terminals and remaining safety/limits are exact-model/manual dependent and left OPEN.

Unlogic’s specific Optimum-branded MH50V manual mill, converted by its owner to LinuxCNC. The owner says he first bought a smaller MH35V, returned it after defects and a long delay, and received the larger MH50V from the reseller. This dossier concerns only the MH50V; the separate German MH50G project mentioned in the thread is not merged with it.

Forum-reported build status: The owner reports completing CNC retrofit commissioning in spring 2024, making a first part, then milling 6082 aluminium with a 12 mm DLC-coated carbide end mill and a FreeCAD adaptive toolpath. By June he said he had been using the mill regularly and liked the finish.

V2 Core route: OPEN. The DB44 drawing is partial; complete Mesa terminal schedule, installed revision, limit/probe wiring and safety-circuit validation are absent. This profile does not establish a fitted V2 conductor or approved wiring.

Delta ASDA-B3 single-phase example · 2026-10-02

Delta manual §3.1.3 (printed p. 3-6 / PDF p. 77) gives a conditional 220 V single-phase example for drives rated at or below 1.5 kW: supply connects across R and T, with S unused. L1C/L2C are shown as a separate control supply; U/V/W connect to the corresponding motor phases. The example shows protective earth at both drive and motor.

Scope: This conditional example is not complete wiring for this MH50V. The existing power panel remains an unresolved installed-model inventory. The exact fitted drive suffix, encoder, and cabinet branch ratings are unresolved. A conditional panel update is being prepared; the current diagram has not been updated.
